FROM (
    SELECT TOP(1) [c].[CartId], [c].[CreatedDate], [c].[ModifiedDate], [c].[UserId]
    FROM [Carts] AS [c]
    WHERE [c].[UserId] = @__userId_0
) AS [c1]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [c0].[CartItemId], [c0].[CartId], [c0].[ProductId], [c0].[Quantity], [c0].[UnitPrice], [p].[ProductId] AS [ProductId0], [p].[CategoryId], [p].[CreatedDate], [p].[Description], [p].[ImageUrl], [p].[IsActive], [p].[ModifiedDate], [p].[Name], [p].[Price], [p].[SKU], [p].[StockQuantity]
    FROM [CartItems] AS [c0]

) AS [s] ON [c1].[CartId] = [s].[CartId]
